  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: [arXiv:2607.05865]已表明：在经验等价于复数量子力学的情形下，该实数构造的"实标志"被隐藏复结构Ĵ保护，复合规则为该结构上的平衡张量积。本文进一步追问：若采取更强的操作解读——把标志视为可访问的实数自由度——会产生什么后果。

Method: 以[arXiv:2601.14638]引入的OD（overlap-determinability）为判据，分析实标志的可读性/相干可控性与射线代表的选取之间的关系，并将其提升为通用的相位访问原语，进而考察其操作后果。

Result: 可读或相干可控的标志会为每条射线固定一个代表，恰好提供了OD所指认的、实现未知态通用叠加所缺失的相位约定资源。该资源一旦被推广为通用相位访问原语，可实现线性相关集的概率克隆（probabilistic cloning）、基于操纵的signaling（steering-based signaling）以及对数查询复杂度的非结构化搜索。

Conclusion: 商空间构造具有明确的后续解读：作为受保护的规范表示，它就是实数记号下的标准复数量子力学；作为可访问的实数理论，它则脱离量子操作框架。

Abstract: Barrios Hita et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 240202 (2026)] proposed a quotient-space formulation of quantum mechanics over the real numbers and concluded that complex numbers are only a convenience. A preceding analysis [arXiv:2607.05865] showed that, when the construction is empirically equivalent to complex quantum mechanics, its real flag is protected by a hidden complex structure $\hat{J}$ and its composition rule is the balanced tensor product over that structure. Here we ask what would follow from the stronger operational reading that the flag is an accessible real degree of freedom. The answer is a direct clash with overlap-determinability (OD), introduced in [arXiv:2601.14638], and with standard no-go theorems. A readable or coherently controllable flag fixes a representative of each ray, and hence supplies precisely the phase convention that OD identifies as the missing resource for universal superposition of unknown states. Once promoted to a generic phase-access primitive, this resource enables probabilistic cloning of a linearly dependent set, steering-based signaling, and logarithmic-query unstructured search. Thus the quotient construction has a sharp follow-up interpretation: as a protected gauge representation it is standard complex quantum mechanics in real notation; as an accessible real theory it leaves the quantum operational framework.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum light sources constitute a crucial physical resource for the construction of quantum networks. Despite remarkable recent progress, there remains a lack of systematic investigation into the bandwidth tunability of quantum light sources under fixed waveguide parameters. In this work, we demonstrate a broadband quantum light source in the 1.5 $μ$m band with tunable bandwidth by changing the temperature of a piece of periodically poled lithium niobate waveguide. In our demonstration, the bandwidth of the quantum light source is tuned from 78.3 nm to 96.2 nm with a temperature change of 1 $^\circ$C . Under different bandwidths, the generation rates of correlated photon pairs are greater than 6.3 MHz with coincidence-to-accidental ratios consistently being no less than 608. The energy-time entanglement properties are measured by using the Franson interference with two-photon interference visibilities larger than 99.06%. Our results provide an effective method for developing the quantum light sources with tunable bandwidth which has great potential for building the large-scale quantum networks.

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有局部分解结构的对数凹分布采样问题，提出量子算法仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\kappa}d)$ 次局部查询，在图模型与稀疏学习方法上优于现有经典与量子结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际问题中目标凸函数 $f(x)=\sum_{a=1}^R \psi_a(x_{S_a})$ 具有局部（条款）分解结构，对单个条款 $\psi_a$ 的求值与梯度查询远比查询整体 $f$ 便宜。已有最优经典算法需 $\widetilde{O}(\kappa d)$ 次查询，而 Childs 等人隐含的量子结果为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\kappa}d^2)$，二者均未充分利用局部结构以降低维度依赖。作者希望证明局部结构可作为量子算法资源，突破现有查询复杂度上限。

Method: 采用量子算法框架，针对强对数凹采样，利用函数的局部条款分解，通过仅访问各条款 $\psi_a(\cdot)$ 的评估与梯度查询来实现采样。关键假设是每个坐标仅出现在少数条款中（低关联度局部结构）。具体技术基于量子查询模型设计采样过程，将查询目标从整体 $f$ 转为结构化局部查询，从而获得对维度 $d$ 的平方根加速。

Result: 在每坐标仅出现在少量条款的条件下，算法以 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\kappa}d)$ 次局部查询完成强对数凹采样，其中 $\kappa$ 为条件数。该复杂度优于经典最优的 $\widetilde{O}(\kappa d)$（Ascolani, Lavenant, Zanella, Ann. Probab. 2026），也优于 Childs 等（NeurIPS 2022）隐含的量子 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\kappa}d^2)$。方法适用于高斯马尔可夫随机场、有限元潜高斯模型与稀疏广义线性模型等广泛局部结构化模型。

Conclusion: 局部结构不仅是实现细节，更是高维采样中的量子算法资源。本文证明局部查询可为强对数凹采样带来显著加速，并适用于统计计算与机器学习中多种代表性结构化模型。

Abstract: For a convex function $f \colon \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$, the problem of sampling from a distribution proportional to $e^{-f(x)}$ is called log-concave sampling. In many practical scenarios, the function $f(x)$ turns out to admit a local decomposition $f(x) = \sum_{a=1}^R ψ_a(x_{S_a})$. In this paper, we consider log-concave sampling using local queries, i.e., evaluation and gradient queries to each clause $ψ_a(\cdot)$, which can be computationally much cheaper than the queries to $f(x)$ itself. We show that if each coordinate appears in only a small number of clauses, there is a quantum algorithm for strongly log-concave sampling using $\widetilde{O}(\sqrtκd)$ local queries, where $κ$ is the condition number. This improves the prior best classical result $\widetilde{O}(κd)$ due to Ascolani, Lavenant, and Zanella (Ann. Probab. 2026) and the quantum result $\widetilde{O}(\sqrtκ d^2)$ implied by Childs et al. (NeurIPS 2022). Our quantum sampler applies to a broad class of locally structured models from statistical computing and machine learning, with representative examples including Gaussian Markov random fields, finite-element latent Gaussian models, and sparse generalized linear models. These results demonstrate that local structure is not merely an implementation detail, but a quantum algorithmic resource for high-dimensional sampling.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a quantum sensor network for direct detection of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion dark matter using entangled superconducting qubits. In the presence of a static magnetic field, the oscillating axion field induces a coherent qubit rotation described by an encoding angle proportional to the axion-photon coupling and an unknown dark-matter phase. Treating the phase as an additional unknown parameter turns axion detection into a two-parameter quantum estimation problem. We formulate these signals in Cartesian coordinates, thereby avoiding the singularity that arises in the weak-signal regime and establishing a regular framework for multiparameter quantum estimation. Using this framework, we optimize the quantum sensor network and combine it with Bayesian inference to reconstruct the axion-photon coupling. The optimized protocol accurately recovers the KSVZ and DFSZ benchmark couplings over the mass range $m_a\in[0.1,10]~μ{\rm eV}$, generalizes to previously unseen masses, and remains robust against realistic superconducting-qubit decoherence and gate errors.

Measured this way, models separate sharply: code models without a reasoning mode solve essentially nothing, and enabling native reasoning on identical weights improves accuracy by an order of magnitude. The failures are overwhelmingly semantic rather than syntactic. Two architectures, a unit-verified constraint agent and a neuro-symbolic compilation pipeline, close most of the remaining gap by delegating correctness-critical composition to deterministic procedures. Ablations quantify the contribution of each component, and resource gating exposes an architecture-dependent trade-off between circuit width and depth. Finally, controlled difficulty escalation reveals architecture-specific responses to difficulty structure: different difficulty axes degrade different methods, while the neuro-symbolic pipeline passes every evaluated instance. The framework turns diagnostic robustness into a tractable control problem.

Abstract: Enhanced performance of quantum reservoir computing has been associated with dynamical phase transitions, but whether this connection extends to dissipative systems and which relaxation mechanisms underlie it remain insufficiently understood. We systematically compare driven-dissipative Kerr reservoirs across first-order and continuous dissipative phase transitions and find enhanced memory and nonlinear processing near both phase boundaries. Although the closure of the Liouvillian gap marks the transitions in the thermodynamic limit, computational performance does not generally follow gap suppression. An exact post-training Liouvillian-mode decomposition quantitatively attributes the trained memory to intrinsic relaxation channels. It shows that the gap mode contributes only weakly, while finite-rate modes and their cross-contributions dominate the enhanced capacity. These results go beyond phenomenological correlations by directly linking memory capacity to the intrinsic Liouvillian relaxation spectrum. Moreover, these findings provide a physical basis for designing dissipative quantum reservoirs and testing memory-enhancement mechanisms in experimentally accessible Kerr platforms.

Result: 得到了初始可分态所生成纠缠的精确解析表达式，并应用于多个确定性图族。弱Katz区域的模体展开显示：三角形在一阶进入，四环、局部度结构和重叠三角形在二阶出现。强传播区域中相互作用由主邻接模及其本征向量的局域化性质主导。对Erdős–Rényi图的弱传播展开可解析平均，稀疏区呈现局部树状贡献，稠密区纠缠距离密度饱和。

Conclusion: 研究结果将纠缠动力学与复杂网络的基于行走（walk-based）和谱的结构建立了联系。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement dynamics of quantum states defined on graphs with non-local Ising interactions governed by the Katz kernel of the underlying network. The interaction pattern is physically motivated by a gapped fermionic mediator propagating on the same graph, whose perturbative elimination yields an effective Katz-weighted Ising Hamiltonian. Using the Entanglement Distance, we derive an exact analytical expression for the entanglement generated from an initially separable state and apply it to representative deterministic graph families.
  We then characterize the dynamics in different propagation regimes. In the weak-Katz regime, the dynamics admits a systematic motif expansion with triangles entering at first order order and four-cycles, local degree structure, and overlappin triangles appearing at second order. In the strong-propagation regime, the interaction is instead dominated by the principal adjacency mode and by the localization properties of its eigenvector. For Erdős--Rényi graphs, the weak-propagation expansion can be averaged analytically, revealing a locally tree-like contribution in the sparse regime and saturation of the Entanglement Distance density in the dense regime.
  Our results connect entanglement dynamics with both the walk-based and spectral structure of complex networks.

</details>

Motivation: 量子计算硬件的发展促使人们寻找能体现"量子优势"的应用，量子多体系统模拟是其中最有前景的方向之一。经典计算机模拟此类系统所需资源随系统尺寸指数增长（根源在于希尔伯特空间的指数增长），而数字量子计算机虽天然在该空间中演化量子态，但其结果通常不能作为确定性输出获得，需依赖受散粒噪声影响的投影测量，期望值必须通过重复测量重构，因此测量次数成为重要的计算资源，对任何量子优势的主张都至关重要。

Method: 以NMR谱模拟为测试案例，研究测量次数资源如何随系统尺寸标度（该标度本身又取决于所需精度）。研究对象包括两类：真实分子，以及一类模型NMR哈密顿量——后者支持具有一维、二维和全连接相互作用的高效大规模模拟。

Result: 所需资源仅随分子尺寸弱增长，远低于底层希尔伯特空间的指数增长。

Abstract: Recent advances in the field of quantum computing hardware motivate the search for applications which demonstrate so-called quantum advantage. One promising use case that has been identified is the simulation of quantum many-body systems. The computational resources required for performing such a simulation on a classical computer generally grow exponentially with the size of the system being modeled, which is ultimately due to the exponential growth of the Hilbert space in which the dynamics of such a system take place. While digital quantum computers natively evolve quantum states directly in such a Hilbert space, thus naively avoiding this problem, the result of such a computation is typically not obtained as a deterministic output. Rather, it requires projective measurements which are fundamentally affected by shot noise. Any desired expectation values must therefore be reconstructed from repeated measurements, making the number of those measurements a relevant computational resource, and thus an important consideration for any potential claims of quantum advantage. In this work, we study how this resource scales with system size (a scaling which itself depends on the desired accuracy of the final result), using the simulation of nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra as a test case. We study this scaling for both real-world molecules, as well as a class of model NMR Hamiltonians which allow for efficient large-scale simulations with one-dimensional, two-dimensional, and all-to-all interactions. We find that the required resources increase only weakly with molecular size, far below the exponential growth of the underlying Hilbert space. This result suggests that shot noise should not pose a fundamental barrier to achieving quantum advantage in the simulation of NMR systems, and perhaps for many-body systems more broadly.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解周期性驱动下哪些初始态能保持记忆，需要超越导致不变结构的理想极限，进入近简并流形中研究。简并本身不足以判定稳定性，真正重要的是扰动（如结构和驱动不完美）作用于投影多重态的方式。

Result: 在周期驱动的清洁短程交替XXZ链中展示了一系列结果：长寿命态（包括乘积形式和跨团簇纠缠形式）、侧带共振的周期族，以及一个明确示例，表明具有相同能量和电荷的态会有相反命运。

Conclusion: 简并本身不是决定性因素；稳定性由扰动在投影多重态上的作用方式决定，具体由耦合连接性和驱动局域选择规则两条判据支配。该框架可预测周期驱动系统中的长寿命记忆态及其共振结构。

Abstract: In order to understand which initial states retain memory under periodic driving, it is necessary to depart from the ideal limit giving rise to invariant structures and work inside near-degenerate manifolds. We show that degeneracy itself is not conclusive and what actually matters is how perturbations, such as structural and driving imperfections, acts on the projected multiplet. For systems decoupling into the local clusters in the ideal limit, the projections can be computed within degenerate perturbation theory. There the stability is decided by two criteria: the coupling can not connect the state with other degenerate partners in the manifold, either by kinetic blocking, or more generally, by diagonalizing the projected coupling, and the drive's local selection rule must admit only transfer energies detuned from Floquet sidebands, in which case the projected drive generator exactly vanishes. We formalize these understandings in a periodically driven clean, short-ranged alternating XXZ chain and demonstrate a set of results that include, long-lived states, both of product form and entangled across the clusters, a periodic family of sideband resonances, and an explicit example showing opposite fates for states with identical energy and charges.

Abstract: Validating variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) pipelines on realistic chemical systems is a critical stepping stone toward tackling classically intractable molecules. A problem-tailored ADAPT-VQE with a full singles-doubles-triples (S+D+T) pool removes over 99% of the correlation error, reaching a stable plateau at 4.03 mEh relative to exact diagonalisation (148 selected operators). This establishes a >40x improvement over the hardware-efficient ansatz (HEA). Finally, we frame these emulator results as a robust baseline for upcoming physical quantum hardware runs on Euro-Q-Exa.

Method: 构建Transformer架构的量子态表征器(TQSC)：由含噪测量数据重构实验制备的纯态与混合态光量子偏振态；利用注意力机制揭示测量可观测量之间的关联，提供物理可解释性；并用Qiskit模拟Bloch球态检验鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: TQSC可在动态噪声下实现精确的量子态层析表征，并提供物理可解释的后验分析，为智能量子信息处理应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum communication underpins secure information processing and scalable quantum networks. In particular, remote state preparation (RSP) enables efficient quantum state transfer, but accurately estimating target states under complex noise remains challenging. Here, we propose a Transformer-based Quantum State Characterizer (TQSC) model for noisy RSP experiments. Our model reconstructs experimentally prepared pure and mixed photonic polarization states from noisy measurements in complex scattering environments, while its attention patterns provide physically grounded insights into correlations among the measured observables. The method achieves a mean estimator-target fidelity exceeding 99.999% under complex scattering and dynamic Gaussian noise, while its robustness and generalization are further examined using Qiskit-simulated Bloch-ball states.Furthermore, in a practical MNIST image transmission task with held-out states, the decoded bit error rate is reduced from 50.34% to zero after TQSC post-processing. The TQSC model enables accurate tomographic characterization under dynamic noise and provides physically grounded post-hoc insights, holding promise for intelligent quantum information processing applications.

</details>


### [87] [Coherent error threshold for quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20537)
*Zhengyi Han,Yuanchen Zhao,Yijia Xu,Yixu Wang,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明通用 qLDPC 码对局域相干噪声（及更一般的局域信道噪声）具有非零码容量阈值，逻辑恢复的钻石距离误差随码距指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC 码对随机 Pauli 噪声的阈值特性已较清楚，但相干误差会产生可干涉的误差叠加，现有理论对其严格理解不足，缺乏一般性鲁棒性保证。

Method: 针对距离 d=Ω(log n) 的 qLDPC 码族，提出"簇重求和"技术：在信道展开中分离出一个大连通误差簇，先对与其不连通的误差精确重求和再取范数，最后用标准簇计数得到指数抑制；结论对最优恢复与最小权重译码器均成立。

Result: 存在常数噪声强度阈值，低于该值时最优恢复与最小权重译码器的逻辑恢复误差（钻石距离）随码距指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了容错理论中的长期难题，为 qLDPC 码抗相干噪声提供通用鲁棒性保证，并为容错量子技术研究奠定严格基础。

Abstract: A key appeal of quantum low-density parity check (qLDPC) codes is their ability to suppress stochastic Pauli noise below nonzero thresholds. Coherent errors are fundamentally different: they produce superpositions of error patterns whose amplitudes can interfere even after syndrome measurement. Rigorous understanding of coherent errors remains limited. Here we show that general qLDPC codes admit a nonzero code capacity threshold against local coherent noise and more generally local channel noise. For any family of qLDPC codes with distance $d=Ω(\log n)$, we show that there is a constant noise strength below which the logical recovery error in diamond distance decays exponentially with the code distance. The result is established for optimal recovery as well as the minimum-weight decoder. The key technical ingredient is what we call a \emph{cluster resummation}: rather than bounding superposed error configurations one by one, we isolate a large connected error cluster in the channel expansion and exactly resum all errors disconnected from it before taking norms. Standard cluster counting then yields exponential suppression. This work resolves a longstanding challenge in fault tolerance theory, providing general robustness guarantees for qLDPC codes against coherent noise and laying a rigorous foundation for future studies of fault-tolerant quantum technologies.

</details>


### [88] [Technical Report OFDM-Assisted Simultaneous Quantum and Classical THz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20542)
*Xin Liu,Chao Xu,Soon Xin Ng,Mohammed El-Hajjar,Phuc V. Trinh,Shinya Sugiura,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在真实频率选择性太赫兹多径衰落信道下，实现经济高效的同时量子与经典通信（SQCC），并提出基于OFDM的SQCC系统，结合LDPC多维QKD协商方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有THz SQCC方案假设单载波传输且信道为平坦衰落，无法适应真实的频率选择性多径衰落环境，因此需要研究更贴近实际的THz SQCC方案。

Method: 通过在叠加相干态上同时传输量子密钥与经典信息，研究光波段和THz波段的SQCC可行性；针对时间不变的频率选择性THz场景，提出基于OFDM的SQCC系统，并采用LDPC编码的多维QKD协商方案支持。

Result: 仿真结果表明，基于OFDM的SQCC方案能够在经典信号与量子信号之间广泛的功率分配场景下实现可用的安全密钥率（SKR）；相比之下，单载波方案在THz SQCC中要求经典信号强度至少比量子信号高100倍。

Conclusion: 基于OFDM的SQCC方案在频率选择性THz信道中具有更好的功率分配灵活性和实用性，优于传统单载波方案。

Abstract: The feasibility of cost-effective simultaneous quantum and classical communication (SQCC) transmitting both the quantum key and classical information via a superimposed coherent state is investigated both in optical and Terahertz (THz) bands. Since the existing THz SQCC schemes assume single-carrier (SC) transmission over flat fading channels, we embark on investigating SQCC in realistic frequency-selective multipath THz fading channels. We then propose an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based SQCC system for time-invariant frequency-selective THz scenarios, supported by low-density parity-check coded (LDPC) multidimensional QKD reconciliation schemes. Our simulation results demonstrate that the OFDM-based SQCC scheme is capable of achieving a practical secret key rate (SKR) over a wide range of power sharing scenarios between the classical and quantum signals. By contrast its single-carrier counterpart requires the classical signal to be at least 100 times stronger than the quantum signal in THz SQCC.

</details>


### [89] [Scalable logical qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20549)
*Matthias Troyer,Chetan Nayak,John Martinis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"可扩展逻辑量子比特"的定义，用于衡量和比较容错量子计算的进展，并从可靠性、规模、能力、性能四个耦合维度加以刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用规模的量子计算需要执行长而复杂的算法，端到端错误率要远低于物理量子比特所能直接支持的水平，因此必须借助纠错后的逻辑量子比特。然而目前缺乏可衡量、可比较的逻辑量子比特进展标准。

Method: 作者给出"可扩展逻辑量子比特"的定义：通过重复量子纠错在长计算中保持的逻辑量子比特，支持带低延迟实时解码与反馈的容错通用操作，并可按应用需求复制到成百上千个。随后沿四个相互耦合的维度（可靠性、规模、能力、性能）对其进行刻画，并讨论这些维度之间的权衡。

Result: 建立了一套以可靠性、规模、能力、性能四个耦合维度为核心的表征框架，用于描述可扩展逻辑量子比特，并明确了各维度间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该定义为衡量和比较逻辑量子比特的进展提供了统一标准，四维耦合框架及其权衡分析有助于指导容错量子计算的发展方向。

Abstract: Utility-scale quantum computing will require executing long, complex algorithms with end-to-end error rates far below what physical qubits can support directly. Error-corrected logical qubits are needed to achieve this goal. To make the progress on logical qubits measurable and comparable we introduce the definition of scalable logical qubits: logical qubits preserved for long computations by repeated quantum error correction, capable of fault-tolerant universal operations with low-latency real-time decoding and feedback, and replicable to the hundreds or thousands, as required by applications. We characterize these scalable logical qubits along four coupled dimensions: reliability, scale, capability, and performance, and discuss trade-offs among these dimensions.

</details>


### [90] [FT-Weave: Real-Time Compilation Framework for Reconfigurable Fault-Tolerant Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20573)
*Wan-Hsuan Lin,Milan Kornjača,Chen Zhao,Sheng-Tao Wang,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FT-Weave：面向容错量子计算的阶段感知实时编译框架，通过运行时协调资源制备、分配、路由与纠错，在两种中性原子早期FTQC架构上实现最高3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模FTQC需在严格硬件与时间约束下协调资源制备、分配、路由与逻辑执行。现有编译方法多基于离线调度和固定的魔态工厂吞吐假设，无法应对随机的资源制备与隐形传态结果，导致执行停顿和硬件利用率不足。

Method: 提出FT-Weave，一个阶段感知（stage-aware）的实时FTQC编译框架，联合协调资源制备、资源分配、隐形传态路由与纠错处理；通过适配运行时资源可用性与硬件约束，使制备、通信与逻辑执行三者重叠。在两种代表性中性原子早期FTQC架构上实例化：横向STAR与T态培育（cultivation）架构。

Result: 在所评估的硬件与延迟模型下，针对二维横场伊辛模型仿真，FT-Weave相较基线编译流程最高取得3倍加速。案例研究进一步发现：最大化暴露的并发度并不必然最小化执行时间——细粒度异步执行虽可减少局部空闲，但其更小的优化窗口与更高的路由争用会抵消收益。

Conclusion: 有效性的关键在于运行时协调而非单纯暴露并行度，这决定了FTQC资源转化为应用吞吐的效率；FT-Weave为跨资源协议与架构的实时编排问题提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) is essential for large-scale quantum computation, but realizing useful application throughput requires coordinating resource preparation, assignment, routing, and logical execution under strict hardware and timing constraints. Many FTQC compilation approaches construct offline schedules using nominal or fixed magic-state factory throughput. Such schedules cannot respond to stochastic resource-preparation and teleportation outcomes, leading to execution stalls and hardware underutilization. In this work, we introduce FT-Weave, a stage-aware real-time FTQC compilation framework that jointly coordinates resource preparation, resource assignment, teleportation routing, and correction handling. By adapting to runtime resource availability and hardware constraints, FT-Weave allows preparation, communication, and logical execution to overlap. We instantiate FT-Weave on two representative neutral-atom, early FTQC architectures: transversal STAR and a T-state cultivation architecture. Under the evaluated hardware and latency model, FT-Weave achieves a speedup of up to 3X over a baseline compilation flow for simulations of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model. In the case study, we further find that maximizing exposed concurrency does not necessarily minimize execution time. Although fine-grained asynchronous execution can reduce local idle time, its smaller optimization windows and increased routing contention can outweigh these gains. Together, these results show that effective runtime coordination, rather then exposed parallelism alone, determines how efficiently FTQC resources translate into application throughput:FT-Weave provides a blueprint for solving this real-time orchestration problem across resource protocols and architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Experimental demonstration of finite-size general security via discrete-modulated CVQKD with real time postprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20596)
*Sven Bodenstedt,Carlos Pascual-García,Nil Canta i Pujol,Martí Sales-Moragues,Mariana Navarro,Pau Gómez Kabelka,Sebastián Etcheverry,Saeed Ghasemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次实验实现离散调制 CVQKD 系统，在约 10^6 轮有限码长下、基于 QPSK 生成可组合安全密钥，可抵御一般攻击，并能在接近商用硬件上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子密钥分发（CVQKD）与电信基础设施兼容，但在实际可用资源下实现可组合安全性一直很困难，对离散调制（DM）协议尤为突出。

Method: 采用 QPSK 调制的 DM CVQKD 系统；安全框架为可变长度、一般安全性框架，依托现代熵积累技术与锥优化；实验流水线支持在近商用硬件上实时运行。

Result: 首次实验演示了面向一般攻击、有限码长（约 10^6 轮）下可生成可组合安全密钥的 DM CVQKD 系统。

Conclusion: 该工作将 DM CVQKD 的可组合安全性与实验可行性结合，证明其可在实用有限码长与近商用硬件条件下实现实时运行，推动 CVQKD 实际部署。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CVQKD) is compatible with telecommunication infrastructure, but implementing composable security with experimentally practical resources has remained challenging, particularly for discrete-modulated (DM) protocols. We report the first experimental demonstration of a DM CVQKD system that generates composable secret keys against general attacks with finite-size block lengths on the order of $\sim 10^{6}$ rounds via a quadrature phase shift keying (QPSK) system. Our implementation follows a variable-length, general security framework enabled by modern entropy accumulation techniques and conic optimization, whose experimental pipeline allows real-time operation on near-commercial hardware.

</details>


### [92] [Proof of Shor's conjecture on the accessible information of quantum dichotomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20600)
*Michele Dall'Arno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文彻底解决了Shor关于量子二分系综可访问信息的猜想：证明任意维度的二分系综，其可访问信息均可由投影测量达到，并给出显式构造与凸优化表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor在近三十年前猜想：任意量子二分系综（两态系综）的可访问信息可由投影测量达到。此前仅有Keil针对qubit情形的证明。该问题长期悬而未决，本文旨在彻底解决这一开放问题。

Method: 1) 基于Fang、Fawzi与Fawzi的近期结果，证明Shor猜想在任意维度成立，并给出自包含的初等证明；2) 对任意给定二分系综与任意测量，显式构造一个投影测量，使其提取的信息优于原测量；3) 将二分系综可访问信息的计算重构为凸问题。

Result: 证明Shor猜想在任意有限维度下成立；给出优于任意给定测量的投影测量的显式构造；并将二分系综可访问信息的计算转化为凸优化问题（尽管一般情况下可访问信息的计算是非凸的）。

Conclusion: Shor猜想被完全证实：二分系综的可访问信息总可由投影测量达到。该工作统一并推广了此前仅限qubit的结果，同时为二分系综可访问信息的高效计算提供了凸优化框架。

Abstract: The accessible information of any given quantum ensemble quantifies the maximum amount of Shannon information that can be extracted from the ensemble by any quantum measurement. Almost three decades ago, Shor conjectured that the accessible information of any quantum dichotomy, that is, an ensemble of two states, is attained by a projective measurement. Recently, a proof of this conjecture restricted to the qubit case was published by Keil. Here, we conclusively settle this longstanding open problem. First, we show that Shor's conjecture follows, in arbitrary dimension, from a recent result by Fang, Fawzi and Fawzi, and we provide a self-contained, elementary proof. Second, for any given dichotomy and measurement, we provide the explicit construction of a projective measurement that outperforms such a measurement in extracting information from the given dichotomy. Third, while the computation of the accessible information is known to be non-convex in general, we frame the computation of the accessible information of dihotomies as a convex problem.

</details>


### [93] [Quantum Noise Limited Nonlinear Phase-Preserving Amplification of a Bosonic Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20644)
*Abel te Riele,Tzula B. Propp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子力学允许存在相位保持且对场算符非线性的确定性放大方案，并刻画了这类噪声受限非线性放大器的完整类别，指出其在Yurke-Stoler猫态与Kerr小猫态最优放大中的用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子力学是否允许同时满足相位空间各角度一致（相位保持）且对底层场算符非线性的确定性放大方案，以及此类方案对量子信息科学是否有实际价值。

Method: 将新提出的放大器与文献中更一般的噪声受限非线性放大器进行对比约束分析，从而对全部噪声受限非线性相位保持放大器进行完整分类与刻画。

Result: 这类放大器确实存在，但其形式相比一般噪声受限非线性放大器受到高度限制；据此完成了该类放大器的完整分类，并确定其具体用途。

Conclusion: 此类相位保持非线性放大器可用于Yurke-Stoler猫态与Kerr小猫态的最优放大，对量子信息科学具有实用价值。

Abstract: Does quantum mechanics allow for deterministic amplification schemes that simultaneously behave uniformly for all angles of bosonic phase space (i.e. phase preserving) and are nonlinear in the underlying field operators? Would such a scheme have some utility to quantum information science? In this paper we answer both questions with a resounding yes. Such amplifiers exist, but their form is highly constrained compared to the more general set of noise limited nonlinear amplifiers previously studied in the literature. This enables us to characterize the entire class of bosonic noise-limited nonlinear phase preserving amplifiers, and identify their utility: namely, the optimal amplification of Yurke-Stoler cat states and Kerr kitten states.

</details>


### [94] [Hardness of Pathfinding in a Welded Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20651)
*David Miloschewsky,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在焊接树上寻找入口到出口路径的量子查询复杂度下界为Ω(2^{n/24})，即不存在高效量子算法能完成该任务，从而回答了该领域的一个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子行走算法能在焊接树上以指数速度超越经典算法找到出口，但"能否高效找到入口到出口的完整路径"一直是未解问题，本文旨在给出否定性回答。

Method: 采用压缩置换oracle（compressed permutation oracle）技术，构造能追踪算法已学习与遗忘的图信息数据库，并证明任何高效量子算法都无法在这些记录中构建入口到出口的路径。

Result: 对于高度为n的树，任何量子查询算法需至少Ω(2^{n/24})次查询才能以常数概率成功。

Conclusion: 焊接树上寻找入口-出口路径不存在高效量子算法，给出了指数级量子查询下界。

Abstract: Starting from the entrance of a welded tree, a quantum walk algorithm can find its exit vertex exponentially faster than any classical algorithm. However, it has been an open question whether any quantum algorithm is able to efficiently find a path from the entrance to the exit. We answer this by proving an exponential quantum query lower bound for finding such path. Specifically, for trees of height $n$, any quantum query algorithm requires at least $Ω(2^{n/24})$ queries in order to succeed with constant probability.
  Our proof uses the compressed permutation oracle technique in order to construct databases which track the graph information an algorithm has learned and forgotten, and show that no efficient quantum algorithm can build an entrance-to-exit path in these records.

</details>


### [95] [TetrisCNN for interpretable detection of phases of matter from experimental quantum simulator data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20693)
*Kacper Cybiński,Björn van Zwol,James Enouen,Guillaume Bornet,Thierry Lahaye,Antoine Browaeys,Antoine Georges,Anna Dawid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出TetrisCNN——一种含多分支异形滤波器的卷积架构，可从实验量子模拟器数据中直接学习稀疏、可解释的潜在表示，并给出基于自旋关联子的符号化相变判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相变探测依赖正确序参量的识别，对未知相变极为困难；现有神经网络方法虽无需先验知识即可定位相变，但属黑箱、只判别相而不阐明其性质，且难以应对真实含噪实验数据。

Method: 提出TetrisCNN，一种带并行分支、采用不同形状滤波器（形似俄罗斯方块）的卷积架构，直接在自旋关联子层面学习稀疏、可解释的潜在表示；应用于二维Ising与XY量子模拟器在多组基下测量的实验快照。

Result: 网络不仅能探测相变与交叉（crossover），还能将其潜在表示和决策边界表达为由实验可测自旋关联子构成的符号公式。

Conclusion: 该框架为将可解释神经网络与量子模拟器结合、进而发现并理解新物相开辟了路径。

Abstract: Detecting phases of matter in general relies on identifying the correct order parameter - a task that remains notoriously difficult for unknown transitions and traditionally is guided by physical intuition and educated guess. Neural networks have recently offered an alternative route by locating phase transitions in known models without any a priori physical knowledge. Yet these approaches remain black boxes and only identify phases without elucidating their properties. Moreover, they often struggle when confronted with realistic, noisy experimental data, which constitute the ultimate testbed for automated methods in physics. Here, we bridge these perspectives by introducing TetrisCNN, a convolutional architecture with parallel branches of differently shaped filters, reminiscent of Tetris blocks, that learns sparse, interpretable latent representations directly in terms of spin correlators. Applied to experimental snapshots of two-dimensional Ising and XY quantum simulators measured in multiple bases, the network not only detects phase transitions and crossovers but also expresses its latent representation and decision boundaries as symbolic formulas built from experimentally measurable spin correlators. This framework opens the way to integrating interpretable neural networks with quantum simulators to uncover and understand new phases of matter.

</details>


### [96] [Multinegativity and single-letter formulas for asymptotic entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20698)
*Raphael Brinster,Tulja Varun Kondra,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入可计算的上界层级来刻画渐近相对熵纠缠，并研究其单字母公式与k-多重负态的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近纠缠量定义在无限多份态上，单字母公式通常难以获得，需寻找可计算且能逼近渐近量的方法。

Method: 构造相对于PPT态的渐近相对熵纠缠的递减可计算上界层级，每一固定层级的正则化等于该渐近量；引入k-多重负态及其k-多重负性推广。

Result: 在两大多参数族（含所有Werner态）中首个非平凡层级具有可加性，给出单字母公式；层级可推广至sandwiched Renyi散度；构造任意深度k的k-多重负态，分离纠缠成本层级的连续层级，反驳有限坍缩猜想。

Conclusion: 得到态依赖的单字母公式，并揭示普适有限层级刻画的局限性。

Abstract: The study of entanglement inevitably leads to the concept of regularization, where measures are evaluated on infinitely many copies of a state. Single-letter formulas try to make these asymptotic entanglement quantities accessible through a calculation on one copy of a state, but are rarely available. We introduce a decreasing hierarchy of computable upper bounds on the asymptotic relative entropy of entanglement with respect to positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The regularization of every fixed hierarchy level equals this asymptotic quantity. Additivity at any level therefore yields a single-letter formula, even when the usual one-copy relative entropy is nonadditive. We establish such additivity at the first nontrivial level for two broad multiparameter families, both containing all Werner states. The upper-bound construction also extends to sandwiched Renyi divergences. The hierarchy motivates $k$-multinegative states, a generalization of binegative states and the associated $k$-multinegativity, which gives explicit upper bounds on both the asymptotic relative entropy and exact PPT entanglement cost. We construct states which are $k$-multinegative at arbitrarily large depths $k$ that separate consecutive levels of the previously introduced entanglement-cost hierarchy, disproving its conjectured finite collapse. These results provide state-dependent single-letter formulas while identifying a limitation of universal finite-level characterizations.

</details>


### [97] [Proof of a positive coherent-error threshold for topological quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20708)
*Shiro Tamiya,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次严格证明了在相干Z旋转错误下，具有有界逻辑量子比特数的量子LDPC码（含表面码等拓扑码）存在正的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错码的阈值分析多基于随机错误模型，但实际器件中的错误可以是相干的（如控制不完美导致的非期望Z旋转），随机模型无法刻画。表面码在相干错误下的阈值行为虽有数值研究支持，却缺乏严格证明。

Method: 结合傅里叶分析以保留相干错误振幅间的干涉效应，并利用抽象聚合物模型的团簇展开（cluster expansion）进行严格分析。

Result: 证明当旋转角低于一个与码尺寸无关的常数时，最大似然Pauli恢复使纠缠不保真度随码距指数衰减，其前置因子与物理量子比特数成线性关系。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了量子纠错的理论基础，为超越随机错误的量子纠错提供了统计力学描述。

Abstract: Threshold analyses of quantum error-correcting codes are well established for stochastic error models, in which errors occur randomly with given probabilities. However, errors in actual devices can also be coherent, such as unwanted $Z$ rotations due to imperfect control, which are not captured by stochastic error models. For the surface code, numerical studies have indicated threshold behavior even under coherent errors, but a rigorous proof of threshold existence is lacking. Here we prove that a positive threshold for coherent $Z$-rotation errors exists for quantum low-density parity-check codes with a bounded number of logical qubits, including the surface code and other topological codes. Specifically, we show that the maximum-likelihood Pauli recovery suppresses the entanglement infidelity exponentially in the code distance up to a prefactor linear in the number of physical qubits whenever the rotation angles lie below a constant that is independent of the code size. The proof combines Fourier analysis to retain the interference among the amplitudes of coherent errors with the cluster expansion of abstract polymer models. Our results expand the theoretical foundation of quantum error correction and offer a statistical-mechanical description of quantum error correction beyond stochastic errors.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Simulation of Dissipative Non-Markovian Coupled Classical Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20721)
*Malte Schade,Sophia Simon,Nathan Wiebe,Scott Keating,Cyrill Bösch,Andreas Fichtner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子算法模拟具非马尔可夫耗散与时变材料属性的经典振子网络，将记忆核用Prony级数近似嵌入马尔可夫态空间，用量子哈密顿模拟线性组合估计能量，查询复杂度为Õ(α_tot·t/ε)；证明该任务最坏情形为BQP完全，并给出适用于微分方程的Lieb-Robinson型界，在三维局域耦合阻尼系统中实现四次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将近期无阻尼谐振系统的量子加速推广到含耗散（黏声、黏弹）介质与材料属性随时间变化的经典振子网络，探索现实阻尼振子网络与近似波动方程获得实用量子加速的可能性。

Method: 用Prony级数近似记忆核，将历史依赖动力学嵌入由非厄米算子支配的马尔可夫态空间；采用哈密顿模拟的线性组合（LCU）估计时刻t部分振子的瞬时动能与势能；并借助一类新的、适用于微分方程的Lieb-Robinson型界分析局域耦合拓扑中指数加速的可行性。

Result: 在误差ε内估计能量需Õ(α_tot·t/ε)次对振子系统的查询，其中α_tot为耗散强度、弹簧常数、逆质量与网络连接稀疏度的多项式；证明该能量估计任务最坏情形经典困难（对应判定问题为BQP完全），即使强耗散下亦然；时变材料属性的变化在能量表象中表现为有效耗散或增长；证明局域耦合拓扑中指数级量子优势不可行；在三维局域耦合阻尼振子系统实现四次（quartic）加速。

Conclusion: 该工作将无阻尼谐振系统的量子加速扩展至非马尔可夫耗散与时变材料介质，明确了经典困难性与局域拓扑下加速上限，为现实阻尼振子网络及近似波动方程的实用量子加速提供了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for simulating classical oscillator networks characterized by non-Markovian dissipation and time-varying material properties, extending recent speedups for undamped harmonic systems to viscoacoustic and viscoelastic media. We embed the history-dependent dynamics into a Markovian state space governed by a non-Hermitian operator by approximating memory kernels through a Prony series. We then use linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation to estimate the instantaneous kinetic and potential energy for a subset of oscillators at time $t$ within error $ε$ using a number of queries to the oscillator system that scales as $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(α_{\rm tot} t/ε)$, where $α_{\rm tot}$ is polynomial in the strength of the dissipation, spring constants, inverse masses, and sparsity of the connections in the network. We further show that this energy estimation task is in the worst-case classically hard (i.e., a corresponding decision problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete), even in the presence of strong dissipation. For time-dependent materials, we show that changes in material properties appear as effective dissipation or growth in the energy representation. Additionally, we prove the infeasibility of exponential quantum advantages in locally coupled topologies through a novel form of Lieb-Robinson-like bounds that apply to differential equations. This allows our quantum algorithms to provide quartic speedups for locally coupled damped oscillator systems in three dimensions, raising the possibility of practical quantum speedups for realistically damped oscillator networks and approximated wave equations.

</details>


### [99] [$k$-fold unbiased measurements and maximal incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20728)
*Sébastien Designolle,Máté Farkas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将k-fold无偏基概念从秩一测量推广到任意秩投影测量，建立k-fold无偏测量(k-UMs)理论，证明秩一与三元测量情形下代数与谱定义的等价性，给出高阶构造的不可能性结果并利用Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数构造无穷多高秩三元组，同时在测量不相容性框架下精确求解3-UMs的广义不相容鲁棒性、构造阈值处噪声版本的联合测量，并给出数值证据表明k-UM噪声阈值可能刻画sum-of-squares层级渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互无偏基刻画了两个量子测量之间的完美互补性，而其向高阶的推广虽已被提出，但本质性的高阶构造几乎完全未知。现有k-fold无偏基仅限秩一基测量，理论受限。作者希望将这一概念推广到任意秩投影测量，以释放更丰富的结构，并为测量不相容性提供精确的度量框架与操作解释。

Method: 作者引入k-fold无偏测量(k-UMs)，将[arXiv:1706.04446]的k-fold无偏基从秩一基测量推广至任意秩投影测量；发展代数k-UMs与谱k-UMs两类定义并研究其等价性；对高阶秩一构造及三结果3-UMs建立no-go结果；利用Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数构造无穷多高秩三元组；在测量不相容性框架下精确求解任意结果数3-UMs的广义不相容鲁棒性，并在相容阈值处显式构造噪声版本的联合测量；最后实现[New J. Phys. 28, 064509 (2026)]中sum-of-squares层级的对称性约化并进行数值验证。

Result: 证明代数与谱k-UMs在秩一测量和三元测量(3-UMs)情形下相互吻合；对高阶秩一构造与三结果3-UMs给出强no-go结果；借助Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数获得无穷多高秩三元组；对任意结果数的3-UMs精确确定广义不相容鲁棒性，并在相容阈值处构造出噪声版本的显式联合测量；数值上以极高精度表明所构造的四结果3-UMs属于最不相容的四结果测量三元组，并给出k-UM噪声阈值可能刻画sum-of-squares层级渐近行为的数值证据。

Conclusion: k-fold无偏测量为高阶互补性提供了较秩一情形更丰富的理论框架：秩一高阶构造普遍受阻(no-go)，而高秩情形可通过Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数实现无穷多构造；3-UMs的不相容鲁棒性可精确解析并在阈值处显式构造联合测量，且其噪声阈值很可能对应sum-of-squares层级渐近行为的最不相容极端，从而在量子测量互补性与不相容性之间建立了定量联系。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases capture perfect complementarity between two quantum measurements. Extensions beyond pairwise unbiasedness have been proposed, but essentially no non-trivial higher-order constructions are known. We introduce $k$-fold unbiased measurements ($k$-UMs), extending the $k$-fold unbiased bases notion of [arXiv:1706.04446] from rank-one basis measurements to arbitrary-rank projective measurements, and show that this higher-rank setting supports a much richer theory. We develop the notions of algebraic and spectral $k$-UMs and prove that they coincide for rank-one measurements and triples of measurements (3-UMs). We establish strong no-go results for higher-order rank-one constructions and three-outcome 3-UMs, but obtain infinitely many higher-rank triples using Hadamard matrices and Clifford algebras. We then give these structures an exact operational interpretation in terms of measurement incompatibility. For 3-UMs with any number of outcomes, we determine their generalised incompatibility robustness exactly and construct an explicit joint measurement for their noisy versions at the compatibility threshold. Finally, we implement the symmetry reduction of the sum-of-squares hierarchy recently introduced in [New J. Phys. 28, 064509 (2026)] and give numerical evidence that the noise thresholds arising from the $k$-UM analysis may characterise the asymptotic behaviour of this hierarchy. In particular, with very high precision, we numerically show that our constructed four-outcome 3-UMs are among the most incompatible triples of four-outcome measurements.

</details>


### [100] [Blind Quantum Computation with a Small Quantum Server](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20729)
*Daniel Lovsted,Filipa C. R. Peres,Joshua Nevin,Selman Ipek,Anne Broadbent*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用Pauli-based computation (PBC)实现盲量子计算，使服务器规模仅依赖于非Clifford门数量，并给出基于纠缠的对偶协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BQC允许低资源客户端安全外包计算，但服务器资源成本随计算规模增长，成为实现瓶颈。

Method: 借助Pauli-based computation (PBC)构造BQC协议，继承PBC的容错性与量子比特虚拟化特性；并提出基于纠缠的对偶协议执行资源态计算。

Result: 服务器规模仅取决于非Clifford门数量；表明即使缺乏经典可模拟性，经典客户端BQC仍可行；对偶协议显著降低执行成本。

Conclusion: PBC为降低BQC服务器资源开销提供了有效途径，并拓展了经典客户端BQC的可行范围。

Abstract: Blind quantum computation (BQC) allows low-resource clients to securely delegate computations to a quantum server, but server resource costs scale with the computation size, posing a bottleneck for implementations. By leveraging Pauli-based computation (PBC), we achieve BQC with a server whose size depends only on the non-Clifford gate count. Our protocol inherits fault tolerance and qubit virtualization from PBC and reveals that classical-client BQC is possible even in the absence of classical simulability. Finally, we present an entanglement-based dual protocol that performs a resource state computation with a dramatically reduced execution cost.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient Non-Uniform Quantum Hermite Transform through Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20739)
*Nitay Mayo,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了在前 N 个振子模式张成空间上的高斯–埃尔米特求积变换，即模式系数与 N 个加权位置空间采样之间的精确基变换，其逻辑门复杂度为 O(N polylog(N,1/ε))，量子宽度为多对数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在振子模式（如量子谐振子）表示与位置空间采样表示之间进行精确且高效的基变换，是量子模拟与相关算法中的基础需求；已有方法在门复杂度或宽度上存在瓶颈，需要同时实现近线性门数与多对数宽度并保证对任意叠加态及辅助寄存器成立的算子误差界。

Method: 利用高斯–埃尔米特求积给出的模式系数与 N 个加权位置空间样本之间的精确基变换关系；通过自适应窗口上的带符号平均，将均匀网格采样转换为加权的埃尔米特根节点采样，并用可变窗口宽度控制放大约束，从而设计出逻辑门复杂度为 O(N polylog(N,1/ε))、量子宽度为多对数级的量子线路实现。

Result: 该变换的实现达到 O(N polylog(N,1/ε)) 的逻辑门复杂度以及多对数的量子宽度；算子误差界 ε 对任意叠加态成立，且涵盖所有辅助寄存器；可变窗口宽度对放大约束的控制带来了近线性复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 通过高斯–埃尔米特求积与自适应窗口带符号平均相结合，可在近线性门复杂度与多对数量子宽度下精确实现前 N 个振子模式的基变换，并给出对任意叠加态及全部辅助寄存器有效的算子误差保证。

Abstract: On the span of the first $N$ oscillator modes, Gauss--Hermite quadrature gives an exact change of basis between mode coefficients and $N$ weighted position space samples. We implement this transform with $O(N\operatorname{polylog}(N,1/\varepsilon))$ logical gates and polylogarithmic quantum width. The operator-error bound $\varepsilon$ holds on arbitrary superpositions and includes all auxiliary registers. The construction uses signed averages on adaptive windows to convert uniform-grid samples into weighted Hermite-root samples. Their varying widths control the amplification cost, giving the near-linear bound.

</details>


### [102] [Mermin-Peres magic rectangles modulo odd primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20746)
*Josse van Dobben de Bruyn,Remy van Dobben de Bruyn,Peter Zeman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文对任意整数 d≥2，构造了 Z/dZ 上既无经典解又存在有限维算子解的线性方程组，推广了 Mermin-Peres 魔方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mermin-Peres 魔方给出了 Z/2Z 上无经典解但有有限维算子解的线性系统。对于奇数的 d，如何构造 Z/dZ 上的类似例子长期未知，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 对每个整数 d≥2 构造 Z/dZ 上的线性系统。对奇素数 p，所用算子作用在两个 p 维 qudit 上，通过在广义 Pauli 群上添加对角多项式相位算子生成一个有限 p-群；经典不一致性由比较阿贝尔子群上赋值的初等线性论证得到。

Result: 对每个整数 d≥2 得到 Z/dZ 上的线性系统，它没有经典解，但具有有限维算子解。对奇素数 p，算子在两个 p 维 qudit 上实现并生成有限 p-群。

Conclusion: 该构造将所有模数的情形统一起来，表明 Mermin-Peres 型量子-经典矛盾并不局限于 Z/2Z，从而将此类无经典解但有算子解的现象推广到任意 Z/dZ。

Abstract: The Mermin-Peres magic square provides a simple example of a system of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ which has no classical solutions but does have a finite-dimensional operator solution. For a long time, it was not known how to construct similar examples over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ with $d$ odd. In this paper, we construct, for every integer $d\ge2$, a linear system over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ that has a finite-dimensional operator solution but no classical solution. For an odd prime $p$, our operators act on two $p$-dimensional qudits and generate a finite $p$-group obtained by adjoining diagonal polynomial phase operators to the generalized Pauli group. Classical inconsistency follows from an elementary linearity argument comparing assignments on abelian subgroups.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum Entropy Contraction and Factorization from Hypercontractivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20753)
*Li Gao,Lijun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了超压缩性蕴含熵收缩，无需细致平衡条件，并给出修正对数Sobolev不等式及相对熵近似张量化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信道中建立超压缩性与熵收缩之间的联系，去除诸如细致平衡、L_p-正则性等较强假设，从而简化并推广先前结果。

Method: 针对关于忠实不变态σ为KMS对称的本原量子Markov半群，通过比较谱隙λ与修正对数Sobolev常数α₁，证明α₁ ≥ λ/(2+log‖σ⁻¹‖∞)，无需L_p-正则性假设。

Result: 证明超压缩性蕴含熵收缩（单信道情形）；得到修正对数Sobolev界α₁ ≥ λ/(2+log‖σ⁻¹‖∞)；作为应用，证明两个条件期望的平均的超压缩性蕴含相对熵的近似张量化。

Conclusion: 该工作去除了对数Sobolev比较中L_p-正则性的假设，建立了更一般的超压缩—熵收缩蕴含关系，并给出条件期望平均的超压缩性到相对熵近似张量化的应用。

Abstract: We prove that hypercontractivity implies entropy contraction for a single quantum channel, without a detailed balance condition. For primitive quantum Markov semigroups that are KMS-symmetric with respect to a faithful invariant state \(σ\), we obtain the modified Log-Sobolev bound $α_1\geq \fracλ{(2+\log\|σ^{-1}\|_\infty)}$ where $λ$ is the spectral gap. This removes the assumption of \(L_p\)-regularity for the comparison through the log-Sobolev constant. As an application, we show that the hypercontractivity of an average of two conditional expectations implies the approximate tensorization of relative entropy.

</details>


### [104] [Procrastinating einselection in non-Markovian quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20757)
*Michael J. Moody,Tara Kalsi,Agung Budiyono,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限维开放量子系统中可观测量的演化推导了量子速度极限，并揭示非马尔可夫性可通过两种机制提升指针态演化速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境诱导超选择（einselection）是理解开放系统与耗散量子动力学中经典性涌现的基础过程，尤其在量子达尔文主义框架下可通过观测量向指针值的演化来研究。已有量子速度极限多针对态本身，缺乏针对有限维时间局域可微开放动力学下可观测量的速度极限，且非马尔可夫性对指针态形成速度的作用尚不清晰。

Method: 针对有限维系统、在可微的时间局域开放动力学下，推导了可观测量演化的量子速度极限。区分并识别非马尔可夫性提升可观测量速度的两种机制：一是由布居保持或复原所支持的直接耗散运动，二是通过恢复可供给相干运动的不对称性而实现的间接增强。

Result: 建立了适用于可观测量的量子速度极限，并证明非马尔可夫性可经由直接耗散（依赖布居保持或复活）与间接相干（恢复不对称性）两条路径加速可观测量的运动。

Conclusion: 在永久性 einselection 情形下，累积的负性（negativity）会延缓指针基的涌现，表明非马尔可夫性对经典性形成具有延迟效应，深化了对指针态形成动力学与非马尔可夫性关系的理解。

Abstract: Among the processes governed by open-system and dissipative quantum dynamics, environment-induced superselection, or einselection, is particularly foundational, since it describes the formation of a preferred set of pointer states through environmental monitoring and ultimately the emergence of classicality. Especially in the context of Quantum Darwinism, einselection can be studied in terms of the motion of an observable towards its pointer value. To this end, we derive a quantum speed limit for observables for finite-dimensional systems governed by differentiable time-local open dynamics. We identify two mechanisms through which non-Markovianity can enhance observable speed: direct dissipative motion supported by population retention or revival, and indirect enhancement through restoration of the asymmetry available for coherent motion. For permanent einselection, this delays the emergence of the pointer basis due to accumulated negativity.

</details>


### [105] [Analytic leakage suppression with a single control field: fast two-qubit gates with tunable couplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20766)
*Lukas Heunisch,Michael J. Hartmann,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Magnus展开的解析脉冲整形方法，仅需单一基带控制通道即可有效抑制超导量子门中的非绝热量子泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准脉冲整形技术（如DRAG和过渡无驱动捷径）通常需要两个正交控制通道，其第二通道用于破坏时间反演对称性，因而无法直接应用于仅有单一实值控制场（如超导电路常用的基带磁通控制）的场景。作者希望找到一种能在单基带通道下实现类似抑制效果的解析方法。

Method: 通过Magnus展开推导出一种简单的解析脉冲整形修正方案，并将其应用于由可调耦合器与基带磁通脉冲实现的双量子比特门（transmon之间）的数值模拟中。

Result: 在真实器件参数下，该修正显著降低了由耦合器快速斜坡引起的非绝热泄漏；在快速iSWAP门中，泄漏被抑制最多达三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法具有通用性，不局限于抑制泄漏跃迁处的无用谱权重，可推广应用于多种量子平台。

Abstract: Simple analytic pulse-shaping techniques are of great practical utility in quantum control, with prime examples being the DRAG method for suppressing leakage in superconducting microwave gates and the transitionless-driving approach to shortcuts-to-adiabaticity. Standard versions of these methods require two orthogonal control channels, with the second channel effectively breaking time-reversal symmetry. This appears to rule out their use in settings with only a single real-valued control field, such as the kind of baseband flux control that is common in many superconducting circuit architectures. We show here that a simple analytic pulse-shaping technique derived via a Magnus expansion is effective even with just a single baseband control channel. We demonstrate its efficacy by simulating a two-qubit gate between transmons realized with a tunable coupler and baseband flux pulses. Our corrections dramatically reduce non-adiabatic leakage caused by ramping the coupler: for realistic device parameters, leakage in a fast iSWAP gate is suppressed by up to three orders of magnitude. Our approach is general, goes beyond simply suppressing unwanted spectral weight at leakage transitions, and can be applied to a variety of platforms.

</details>


### [106] [All causally separable quantum processes are quantum circuits with classical control of causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20774)
*Julian Wechs,Alastair A. Abbott,Cyril Branciard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了多体因果可分离性判据的公开问题，证明已有充分条件亦为必要条件，从而给出多体因果可分离性的完整刻画，并表明所有因果可分离过程都可实现为因果顺序经典可控的广义量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果(非)可分离性刻画执行局域量子操作的各方之间因果顺序是否良定义或不定。该概念已被推广至一般多体情形，但此前工作仅给出了多体因果可分离性的充分条件，其是否必要仍是公开问题。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，建立完整判据。

Method: 提出一种新颖的"相干隐形传态技术"（coherent teleportation technique），用以证明Wechs、Abbott与Branciard所识别的充分条件同时具有必要性。

Result: 证明了该充分条件也是必要条件，因而构成多体因果可分离性的完整刻画。作为推论，所有因果可分离过程均可实现为操作顺序受经典控制的广义量子电路。

Conclusion: 多体因果可分离性由此获得完整表征，且全部因果可分离过程都容许"因果顺序经典可控的量子电路"这一实现形式。

Abstract: The concept of causal (non)separability describes whether the causal order between parties that perform local quantum operations is well-defined or indefinite. Causal (non)separability in the general multipartite setting was introduced and studied in [Oreshkov and Giarmatzi, New J. Phys. 18, 093020 (2016); Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)]. We resolve an open problem from these earlier works by showing -- using a novel "coherent teleportation technique" -- that a sufficient condition for causal separability identified in [Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)] is also necessary, and thus provides a complete characterisation of multipartite causal separability. A consequence of this result is that all causally separable processes admit a realisation as generalised quantum circuits in which the order between the operations is classically controlled, known as "quantum circuits with classical control of causal order".

</details>


### [107] [Asymptotically Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20780)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了量子比特上的渐近优质量子局部可测试码（qLTC）的显式族：显式量子LDPC CSS码，具有常数码率、常数相对距离，以及常数权重、常数soundness的局部测试器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造同时具备常数码率、常数相对距离和常数soundness局部测试器的显式量子局部可测试码（qLTC）是量子编码与复杂性理论中的核心难题，此前缺乏在量子比特上显式达到渐近优的构造。

Method: 在量子比特上构造显式的量子LDPC CSS码族，使其满足常数码率、常数相对距离，并配备常数权重的局部测试器，且该测试器具有常数soundness。

Result: 得到显式的渐近优量子局部可测试码族，即在量子比特上同时实现常数码率、常数相对距离和常数soundness，并保持测试器权重为常数。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了量子比特上渐近优qLTC的显式构造，推进了量子LDPC码与量子局部可测试码的研究。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes over qubits. More precseily, we construct explicit quantum LDPC CSS codes over qubits having constant rate, constant relative distance and constant weight local testers with constant soundness.

</details>


### [108] [Parallel quantum channel discrimination and numerical ranges in tensor product subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20781)
*Adam Bílek,Paulina Lewandowska,Ryszard Kukulski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行方案下的完美量子信道分辨，借助数值域（numerical range）给出SDP结合二分法的算法，在关于拷贝数线性的时间内计算完美分辨所需的量子态，并确定所需最小拷贝数，从而肯定地解决了Duan等人的猜想1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道分辨在量子信息论中至关重要，其中信道可被"完美"分辨的情形尤受关注。已有工作（Duan、Guo、Li和Li的猜想1）刻画了某一类算子子空间在并行使用下完美分辨所需的拷贝数，但该猜想尚未被证明，且缺乏高效计算完美分辨态与最小拷贝数的方法。

Method: 采用并行方案研究完美量子信道分辨。发展了一个半定规划（SDP）表述，并结合二分法（bisection）来构造用于完美分辨的量子态。全部结果基于数值域的概念及其基本性质，核心是证明并利用如下关键事实：矩阵子空间张量积的数值域的最小角等于各子空间数值域最小角之和。

Result: 所提算法在关于拷贝数线性的时间内计算出用于完美分辨的量子态；同时得到了实现完美分辨所需的最少量子信道拷贝数；据此肯定地解决了Duan、Guo、Li和Li（arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016）的猜想1。

Conclusion: 通过数值域及其最小角在张量积下的可加性，本文为并行完美量子信道分辨提供了高效算法与最小拷贝数刻画，并证实了相关猜想。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination plays a crucial role in quantum information theory. Of particular interest is the case in which the channels can be discriminated perfectly. In this work, we focus on the perfect quantum channel discrimination task in a parallel scheme. We develop an SDP formulation combined with a bisection procedure to compute a quantum state for perfect discrimination in time linear in the number of copies. In addition, we obtain the minimal number of copies of quantum channels to achieve perfect discrimination. Thanks to that, we settle in the affirmative Conjecture 1 of Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016], which characterizes the number of parallel uses needed to discriminate perfectly a distinguished family of operator subspaces. All our results are obtained using the notion and basic properties of the numerical range. In particular, the key fact that we prove and use is that the minimal angle of the numerical range of a tensor product of matrix subspaces equals the sum of the minimal angles of the numerical ranges of the individual subspaces.

</details>


### [109] [Locally optimized variational evolution for quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20802)
*Carolin Wille,Max Marvell,Lauren Stewart,Max Murphy,Vinul Wimalaweera,Lesley Gover,Andrew G. Green*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种变分时间演化原理，用局域约化密度矩阵上的代价函数替代全局态保真度，从而在多体动力学中利用热化带来的局域复杂度降低，兼顾短时相干动力学与长时简化。该原理可导出类似 TDVP 的闭式运动方程（局部优化 MPS），并具有量子-经典对应形式，在 Quantinuum H2 与 IBM Heron 处理器上验证了特征局域动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子优势依赖全局波函数的指数级复杂度，但局域可观测量并不继承这种全局复杂度，而是受与系统总尺寸无关的内禀局域复杂度支配。尤其在热化系统中，局域可观测量会丢失微观细节并弛豫到由少数参数决定的平衡值。现有方法以全局态保真度为代价衡量演化，未能利用这一区别，因此需要一种只针对局域信息的变分演化框架。

Method: 引入变分时间演化原理：以局域约化密度矩阵上定义的代价函数取代全局态保真度作为优化目标。具体算法基于对矩阵乘积态（MPS）的局部优化，导出类似时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）的闭式运动方程。进一步给出该变分原理的量子-经典对应版本：局域代价由量子硬件评估，配合对散粒噪声与硬件噪声鲁棒的优化策略。

Result: 该演化在短时保持相干动力学，在长时利用热化带来的简化。在 Quantinuum H2 与 IBM Heron 处理器上的原理验证实现成功复原了特征局域动力学。

Conclusion: 通过将变分原理的关注点从全局态保真度转向局域约化密度矩阵，可在热化系统中有效降低模拟复杂度，同时保留短时相干演化。所提 MPS 局部优化算法与量子-经典混合方案具备对噪声的鲁棒性，并在真实量子处理器上得到验证，为利用局域复杂度实现量子优势提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Conventional quantum advantage in many-body dynamics is based on avoiding the simulation cost on a classical computer that arises from the extensive exponential complexity of the global wavefunction. Local observables, however, do not inherit this extensive complexity and may instead be governed by an intrinsic local complexity that is independent of the total system size. This distinction is particularly relevant in thermalising systems, where local observables lose memory of microscopic details and relax towards equilibrium values determined by only a few parameters. Here we introduce a variational time-evolution principle that exploits this distinction by replacing global-state fidelity with a cost function defined on local reduced density matrices. The resulting evolution retains coherent short-time dynamics while exploiting the simplification produced by thermalisation at later times. The concrete algorithm we propose is based on locally optimising matrix-product states and admits closed-form equations of motion analogous to the time-dependent variational principle. We show that the same variational principle has a quantum-classical counterpart, combining quantum evaluation of the local cost with an optimisation strategy robust to both shot and hardware noise. Proof-of-principle implementations on Quantinuum H2 and IBM Heron processors recover the characteristic local dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Environment Alignment and Redundant Record Formation in Imperfect-CNOT Quantum Darwinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20823)
*Aleksander Lasek,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究不完备CNOT模型（含随机高斯耦合、纯净非相互作用环境量子比特）中，制备、相互作用角度与场无序如何共同影响量子达尔文主义的冗余记录形成；提出用条件分支分离几何解释非单调记录行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通过环境冗余记录解释客观信息。已有工作表明不完备记录可被放大，且环境自演化可增强或抑制记录形成，但制备、相互作用角度与场无序如何联合影响记录尚不清楚。

Method: 构建含随机高斯耦合的不完备CNOT模型，环境为纯净非相互作用量子比特；引入单一对准参数Λ刻画制备与相互作用角度依赖；分析条件态可区分性、Holevo信息、局部场（均匀与高斯分布，等均方根强度）及片段信息；结合片段信息精确表达式与最多24个环境量子比特的数值模拟。

Result: 无场时单一对准参数Λ决定条件态可区分性的制备与相互作用角度依赖；增大相互作用不完备度或局域场强度可改善或抑制记录；通过条件分支分离几何解释非单调响应；纯初态下Z基对Holevo信息最优，故场辅助记录无需改变被记录可观测量；等均方根下高斯分布场相比均匀场会展宽有益与有害场效应；区分高平均信息与可靠记录，并关联片段大小需求。

Conclusion: 结果将局部信息获取几何与稳健记录所需片段大小联系起来；分支可区分性分析仅依赖各环境量子比特的条件演化，可推广至其他保持系统指针基的相互作用。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains objective information through redundant environmental records. Earlier work established that imperfect records can be amplified and that environment self-evolution can enhance or suppress their formation. We investigate how preparation, interaction angle, and field disorder combine in an imperfect-CNOT model with random Gaussian couplings and pure, noninteracting environment qubits. We find that, without fields, a single alignment parameter $Λ$ determines the preparation and interaction-angle dependence of conditional-state distinguishability. Increasing interaction imperfection or local field strength can improve or suppress recording. We explain this nonmonotonic response through the geometry of conditional branch separation. For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>
