### [111] [Si- and C-Induced Modifications in Vapor-Solid-Grown InGaAs Nanowires: From Crystal Structure to Carrier Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19332)
*Hamidreza Esmaielpour,Leopold Rothmayer,Thomas Trinkl,Laura Niermann,Tore Niermann,Michael Lehmann,Jonathan J. Finley,Gregor Koblmüller*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 研究了Si与C掺杂对无催化剂选区MBE生长InGaAs纳米线的结构、形貌及光学性质的影响，揭示掺杂种类与载流子动力学的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III-V族三元纳米线在下一代光电子与光子器件中应用广泛，但气相-固相（VS）生长的InGaAs纳米线中，掺杂种类、晶体结构与载流子动力学之间的相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）在无催化剂、选区生长的条件下制备Si和C掺杂的InGaAs纳米线，结合扫描电子显微镜、光致发光光谱及时间分辨光致发光光谱等手段表征其结构、形貌与光学特性，并考察热载流子效应。

Result: C掺杂使纳米线长径比较未掺杂样品提升超过100%，同时平面缺陷密度降低，晶体质量提升；但C掺杂纳米线光致发光强度减弱，源于点缺陷与表面复合速率增加，时间分辨光致发光证实非辐射复合加速。Si与C掺杂纳米线中均可观测到热载流子效应特征，但强度降低。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了掺杂参数与材料特性及载流子动力学之间的明确关联，为理解VS生长InGaAs纳米线的基本机制及其光电子器件应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Determining the impact of dopants in ternary III-V nanowires (NWs) is crucial for their applications in next-generation optoelectronic and photonic devices; however, the interplay between dopant species, crystal structure, and carrier dynamics in vapor-solid-grown InGaAs NWs remains underexplored. In this study, the influence of silicon (Si) and carbon (C) doping on the structural, morphological, and optical properties of catalyst-free, selective-area grown InGaAs NWs fabricated by molecular beam epitaxy is investigated. Scanning electron microscopy demonstrates that C-doping increases the NW aspect ratio by over 100% compared to undoped NWs, while a concomitant decrease in their planar defect density is observed, indicating enhanced crystal quality with C-doping. Despite their superior crystal quality, the C-doped NWs present a diminished photoluminescence intensity, due to point defects and increased surface recombination velocity. This is further supported by time-resolved photoluminescence spectroscopy, revealing accelerated non-radiative recombination in the C-doped NWs. Lastly, hot-carrier effects in these nanostructures are studied, and their signatures remain observable, albeit with reduced intensity in both Si- and C-doped NWs. This comprehensive study establishes clear correlations between doping parameters and the resulting material characteristics and carrier dynamics, offering valuable insights into the fundamental mechanisms governing the VS-grown InGaAs NWs and their implications in optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [112] [Static-to-dynamic field conversion in a temporally switched Lorentz medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19373)
*Alazar G. Salfore,Mario J. Mencagli*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 研究时间突变洛伦兹介质中静电场到动态电磁场的转换，基于拉普拉斯域解析求解初值问题，揭示色散与材料记忆对瞬态场生成和演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究初始处于静电场的时间开关洛伦兹介质中，静态电磁状态如何通过突变转化为瞬态辐射，以及材料色散与记忆在此过程中的作用。

Method: 对振荡强度突变引发的初值问题在拉普拉斯域建模，利用有限洛伦兹平板的极点解析表示生成场，分析色散导致的多模结构（含向洛伦兹极化动态奇点累积的板模极点族）。

Result: 色散使模态结构比非色散情形更丰富；材料记忆决定瞬态最早阶段，产生平滑场起始并以高频介电常数设定最早传播扰动的速度；宽带频谱使瞬态采样后开关响应的正、近零与负介电常数区域，形成定性不同的空间场分布。

Conclusion: 材料色散与记忆从本质上塑造了由初始静态电磁状态产生辐射的生成与演化过程。

Abstract: We investigate static-to-dynamic electromagnetic field conversion in a temporally switched Lorentz medium initially subjected to an electrostatic field. An abrupt change of the oscillator strength drives the material away from its initial equilibrium, while the polarization retains memory of the pre-switch state and acts as the source of the resulting transient. We formulate the resulting initial-value problem in the Laplace domain and derive an analytical representation of the generated field in terms of the poles of the finite Lorentz slab. Material dispersion gives rise to a richer modal structure than in the nondispersive case, including families of slab-mode poles that accumulate toward the singularities associated with the Lorentz polarization dynamics. We further show that material memory governs the earliest stage of the transient, producing a smooth field onset and setting the velocity of the earliest propagating disturbance through the high-frequency permittivity. Because the temporal switch generates broadband spectral content, the transient samples positive-, near-zero-, and negative-permittivity regions of the post-switch material response, leading to qualitatively different spatial field distributions. These results reveal how material dispersion and memory fundamentally shape the generation and evolution of radiation produced from an initially static electromagnetic state.

</details>


### [113] [Giant Resonant Reflection Gain from Injection-Induced Quenching in a Tunnel Diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19494)
*Darmindra Arumugam,Jack Bush,Brook Feyissa*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 负阻微波振荡器在2.96 GHz实现同步诱导相位局域化，获得83.1 dB的巨大谐振反射增益，源于相位涨落的同步淬灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负阻微波振荡器既能维持自主振荡又能相干散射电磁波，可实现超越传统线性放大的有源反射；本文旨在揭示其同步诱导相位局域化带来的巨大谐振反射增益机制。

Method: 在约2.96 GHz的自持隧道二极管振荡器上施加弱谐振射频注入，观测从宽带自由运行态到相位局域窄带反射载波的转变；采用时间分辨谱图解析注入锁定与频率牵引，并用含噪Stuart-Landau模型模拟；通过宽带偏置噪声人为增大相位扩散进行验证。

Result: 反射增强相对注入信号达83.1 dB，伴随超过一个数量级的线宽塌缩、非线性注入牵引及15.84 kHz的同步带宽；时间分辨谱图直接分辨注入锁定与频率同步；含噪Stuart-Landau模拟复现了谱集中及Hopf失稳附近随失谐的增益滚降；增大相位扩散会抑制反射增强。

Conclusion: 证实同步诱导的相位涨落淬灭是巨大相干反射增益的根源，相位局域化是负阻振荡器实现有源反射增强的核心机制。

Abstract: Negative-resistance microwave oscillators can simultaneously sustain autonomous oscillations and coherently scatter electromagnetic waves, enabling active reflection beyond conventional linear amplification. Here we demonstrate giant resonant reflection gain from synchronization-induced phase localization in a self-sustained tunnel diode oscillator operating near 2.96 GHz. Weak resonant RF injection drives a transition from a broadband free running state to a phase localized narrowband reflected carrier. The resulting reflected enhancement reaches 83.1dB relative to the injected signal and is accompanied by greater than order of magnitude linewidth collapse, nonlinear injection pulling, and a synchronization bandwidth of 15.84 kHz. Time resolved spectrograms directly resolve injection locking and frequency entrainment, while noisy Stuart Landau simulations reproduce the observed spectral concentration and detuning dependent gain roll off near the Hopf instability. Artificially increasing phase diffusion with broadband bias noise suppresses the reflected enhancement, confirming synchronization-induced quenching of phase fluctuations as the origin of the giant coherent reflection gain.

</details>


### [114] [Energy Harvesting for Self-Powered Microsystems: A Critical Review of Materials, Power Management, and System Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20157)
*Lyuye Lin,Feng Zhifu,Ermanno Miele,Giuseppe Cantarella,Elena Degoli,Diego Angeli,Lethy Krishnan Jagadamma,Feng Gao,Michele Magno,Ivano Eligio Castelli,Tommaso Ongarello,Chunbo Liu,Roman Krahne,Denis Garoli,Remo Proietti Zaccaria*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了能量收集技术（TENG、压电/热释电、室内光伏、RF整流天线及多源混合集成）的最新突破，并重点批判性地揭示了材料层面之外的“隐形”系统级瓶颈：超低压冷启动PMIC、混合能源阻抗匹配、以及微超级电容器/薄膜电池在实际工况下的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT、可穿戴生物电子与信息物理基础设施的激增，使传统电化学电池成为长期免维护自主微系统最主要制约。为实现永续、无电池运行，能量收集成为变革性范式。现有研究多聚焦材料创新，忽视系统级瓶颈，本文旨在弥补这一缺口。

Method: 综述性综合分析：聚焦摩擦纳米发电机（TENG）、压电/热释电换能器、室内光伏、射频整流天线及其多源混合集成；批判性审视超低压冷启动电源管理集成电路、混合能源阻抗匹配及微储能器件退化等系统级问题，而非停留于材料中心叙事。

Result: 总结多项范式转变性突破：消除机械磨损的固-液TENG；将工作带宽拓宽超300%的非线性压电振荡器；面向高电荷密度介电材料的机器学习加速发现；基于超材料架构的多频段与宽带RF收集。

Conclusion: 能量收集向永续无电池微系统的发展，其关键限制已从材料性能转向系统级集成与可靠性。未来研究须同等重视超低压冷启动PMIC、多源阻抗匹配与微储能器件在真实工况下的退化，方能真正实现长期免维护自主运行。

Abstract: The relentless proliferation of the Internet of Things (IoT), wearable bioelectronics, and cyber-physical infrastructure has rendered the conventional electrochemical battery the single most prohibitive bottleneck to long-term, maintenance-free autonomous microsystems. Energy harvesting, i.e. the conversion of ambient mechanical, thermal, and radiative energy into usable electrical power, has consequently emerged as a transformative paradigm to realize perpetual, battery-independent operation. This review critically synthesizes the most significant advances in energy harvesting technologies over the recent period, with a focus on triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs), piezoelectric and pyroelectric transducers, indoor photovoltaics, radio-frequency (RF) rectennas, and their multi-source hybrid integrations. We highlight paradigm-shifting breakthroughs, including liquid-solid TENGs that eliminate mechanical wear, nonlinear piezoelectric oscillators that broaden operational bandwidth by over 300%, machine-learning-accelerated material discovery for high charge-density dielectrics, and multiband and broadband RF harvesting enabled by metamaterial architectures. Crucially, we move beyond conventional materials-centric narratives to critically interrogate the "unseen" system-level bottlenecks: ultra-low-voltage cold-start power management integrated circuits (PMICs), the impedance-matching challenges of hybrid energy sources, and the persistent degradation of micro-supercapacitors and thin-film batteries under realistic field conditions.

</details>


### [115] [Three-dimensional vector waves in time-variant materials, with applications in time-reversed focusing and Green's function retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20357)
*Kees Wapenaar,Dirk-Jan van Manen*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文为均匀时变介质中的三维电磁与弹性动力学矢量波传播与散射提出了统一波动方程，并基于其对称性导出了净场动量密度守恒、因果与非因果格林函数间的互易关系，进而推广了时间反转聚焦与格林函数提取理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于时变材料中波传播与散射的研究大多局限于由标量波动方程描述的二维波，缺乏对三维电磁与弹性动力学矢量波的统一处理框架，且经典时间反转聚焦与格林函数提取理论在时变介质中的对应形式尚未建立。

Method: 引入一个适用于均匀时变介质中三维电磁与弹性动力学矢量波传播和散射的统一波动方程；利用该方程的对称性质进行理论推导，建立净场动量密度守恒定律以及因果格林函数与非因果格林函数之间的互易关系，并将该互易关系用于推导时变介质中经典时间反转聚焦与格林函数提取的对应形式。

Result: 建立了统一波动方程；证明了净场动量密度守恒；导出了因果与非因果格林函数间的互易关系；基于该关系得到了时变介质中时间反转聚焦与格林函数提取的推广形式。

Conclusion: 该统一波动方程框架将时变介质中矢量波的研究由二维标量情形推广至三维电磁与弹性动力学情形，所导出的守恒律与互易关系为时变介质中的时间反转聚焦和格林函数提取提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The behavior of wave propagation and scattering in time-variant materials has been studied by many authors, for electromagnetic as well as for mechanical waves. Most studies consider two-dimensional waves, governed by scalar wave equations. Here we introduce a unified wave equation for three-dimensional electromagnetic and elastodynamic vector-wave propagation and scattering in homogeneous, time-variant materials. Using the symmetry properties of this equation, we establish the conservation of net field-momentum density, and a reciprocity relation between causal and acausal Green's functions. The latter relation is exploited in the derivation of the counterparts of classical time-reversed focusing and Green's function retrieval for time-variant materials.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [A State-Space Model of Figured-Bass Realization: Local Constraints, Coupled Voices, and Polynomial-Time Solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19397)
*Evan Unit Lim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文将"考试风格"的四声部数字低音实现建模为分层图上的路径问题，把音高拼写、音域、和弦音、重复、省略、间距、交叉、重叠、旋律进行、连续完全音程及解决规则等表示为谓词，区分硬约束与可选偏好代价，并证明在固定声部数与有限音高域下，可行性与最小代价实现均为多项式时间问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字低音实现本质上是一系列受约束的选择，既受单个和声音响内部约束，也受相邻和声音响之间的约束。作者希望为受限的、考试风格的四声部实现问题给出一个显式的数学模型，将传统和声规则形式化为可计算的约束，并厘清哪些是必须满足的硬约束、哪些是可权衡的偏好代价。

Method: 将每个四音和弦（四个带标签的音）表示为一个有序的声部状态，并可视化为四边形的四个顶点。把音高拼写、音域、和弦成员资格、重复、省略、间距、声部交叉、声部重叠、旋律进行、连续完全音程以及若干解决要求全部表达为谓词，并区分硬约束与可选的偏好代价。合法的和声进行被编码为分层图中的路径，从而将可行性与最小代价实现问题转化为图上的路径判定与最短路径问题。对复杂度进行分析，并给出二拍、四拍、八拍的具体算例。

Result: 证明了当声部数固定、音高域有限且局部规则固定时，可行性与最小代价实现均可在多项式时间内求解；在音域固定、音高字母表固定且规则仅涉及相邻事件时，图操作次数关于事件数量呈线性。算例展示了合法性判定、优化过程，以及贪心选择可能失败的情况。

Conclusion: 该结果针对所提出的形式化模型，并不声称能够捕捉全部音乐判断；作者明确承认局部谓词无法涵盖所有音乐性评判，因此结论的适用范围限于该显式模型之内。

Abstract: Figured-bass realization can be described as a sequence of choices constrained both within each sonority and between successive sonorities. This paper gives an explicit mathematical model of a restricted, examination-style four-part realization problem. Pitch spelling, range, chord membership, doubling, omission, spacing, crossing, overlap, melodic motion, consecutive perfect intervals, and selected resolution requirements are expressed as predicates. We distinguish hard constraints from optional preference costs. Four labeled notes are represented visually as the vertices of a quadrilateral and computationally as one ordered voicing state. Legal progressions become paths through a layered graph. We prove that feasibility and minimum-cost realization are polynomial-time problems for a fixed number of voices with explicit finite note domains and fixed local rules. For fixed ranges, a fixed note alphabet, and adjacent-event rules, the number of graph operations is linear in the number of events. Worked two-, four-, and eight-beat examples illustrate legality, optimization, and the failure of a greedy choice. The result concerns the stated formal model; it is not a claim that every musical judgment is captured by local predicates.

</details>


### [117] [A Cross-Lingual Acoustic Disease-Alignment Framework for Respiratory Health Assessment from Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19398)
*Roksana Khanom,Raghib Asfak Tasnim,Bodrun Nahar Bithi,Shafia Shirin Supty,Saiful Islam Raju,Ashok Agrawala,Nirupam Roy*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨语言疾病对齐框架CL-DAF，用于从自发性语音中提取跨语言一致的呼吸疾病声学特征，从而提升多语言COPD检测的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发性语音是一种可扩展、非侵入性的呼吸健康评估信号，但疾病相关的声学变化会被语言特有的语音变异所混淆，导致可解释且能跨语言泛化的模型难以构建。

Method: 使用201名英语和75名新采集的孟加拉语说话人，构建272维公共声学表示；利用有符号秩双序列效应量和语言不变性得分量化疾病对齐；提出CL-DAF框架筛选跨语言疾病效应一致的声学维度。

Result: 孟加拉语自发性语音可区分COPD与对照（AUC 0.85）；但133个特征在跨语言间疾病方向反转，完整表示的跨语言迁移效果差（孟加拉语到英语AUC 0.49）；CL-DAF筛选出26个疾病对齐特征，使英语到孟加拉语和孟加拉语到英语的AUC分别提升至0.825和0.722。

Conclusion: 该研究为强调病理特征而非语言依赖变异的多语言临床语音模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Spontaneous speech offers a scalable, noninvasive signal for respiratory health assessment, yet interpretable models that generalize across languages remain challenging because disease-related acoustic changes are confounded by language-specific phonetic variation. We present CL-DAF, a Cross-Lingual Disease-Alignment Framework that identifies acoustic dimensions whose disease effects remain consistent across languages. Using 201 English and 75 newly collected Bangla speakers, we construct a common 272-dimensional acoustic representation and quantify disease alignment using signed rank-biserial effects and the Language Invariance Score. We first show that spontaneous Bangla speech separates COPD from controls (AUC 0.85); however, 133 features reverse their disease direction across languages and the full representation transfers poorly (AUC 0.49 from Bangla to English). CL-DAF isolates 26 disease-aligned features that raise AUCs to 0.825 and 0.722 from English to Bangla and Bangla to English, respectively. These findings provide a foundation for multilingual clinical speech models emphasizing pathology over language-dependent variation.

</details>


### [118] [CoRELoop: Parameter-Efficient Controlled Recurrent Refinement for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19818)
*Kunyu Feng,Yuxiang Wang,Li Wang,Wan Lin,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CoReLoop，一种在冻结的SSL音频深伪检测器上通过轻量循环细化模块实现有效循环精化的方法，无需额外数据或修改原参数，在14个跨域测试集上将EER从4.85%降至3.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频深伪检测器对未见攻击的泛化仍具挑战，且收集覆盖所有潜在攻击的训练数据不切实际，因此需要在不增加数据、不改动已训练检测器原始参数的前提下提升泛化能力。作者尝试在已训练的SSL检测器上进行循环精化，但发现直接复用编码器输出作为输入会导致检测性能下降。

Method: 提出CoReLoop：通过适配循环输入以匹配冻结编码器、控制状态更新、并将精化输出与冻结分类器对齐，使输出复用变得有效。仅训练轻量细化模块和针对循环的低秩适配器（LoRA），且仅使用原始数据，从而在保留检测器原始首轮预测的同时实现额外精化。并引入可选的中止头（halting head）为每个语音样本自适应选择循环深度。

Result: 在14个跨域测试集上，24层模型经两轮循环将汇总EER从4.85%降至3.74%，可训练参数约10M（总参数598M）。加入中止头后，以平均1.18轮循环达到3.73%的汇总EER。

Conclusion: 通过轻量循环细化与冻结检测器的协同设计，可在不改变原始参数、不增加数据的条件下显著提升音频深伪检测的跨域泛化能力，且自适应循环深度能在几乎不损失精度的前提下降低计算开销。

Abstract: Generalizing to unseen attacks remains challenging for audio deepfake detectors, and collecting training data covering all potential attacks is impractical. We explore recurrent refinement in an already-trained SSL-based detector without additional data or changes to its original parameters. However, directly recycling encoder outputs as inputs degrades detection in our diagnostic. We propose CoReLoop, which makes this reuse effective by adapting recurrent inputs to the frozen encoder, controlling state updates, and aligning refined outputs with the frozen classifier. By training only lightweight refinement modules and loop-specific low-rank adapters on the original data, CoReLoop enables additional refinement while preserving the detector's original first-pass prediction. On 14 cross-domain test sets, the 24-layer model reduces pooled equal error rate (EER) from 4.85% to 3.74% with two passes, with approximately 10M trainable parameters out of 598M. To selectively apply this refinement, an optional halting head chooses the depth for each utterance, achieving 3.73% pooled EER with an average of 1.18 passes.

</details>


### [119] [Robust Workflow Generation via Adversarial Learning for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20063)
*Xiang Li,Pin-Yu Chen,Wenqi Wei*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 ROGUE 框架，通过双智能体对抗学习动态构建鲁棒的音频深度伪造检测工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成与语音转换技术快速发展使音频深度伪造愈发逼真，带来严重安全风险。现有检测方法在受控条件下表现良好，但在真实世界的扰动与损坏下往往泛化能力不足。

Method: ROGUE 将工作流生成建模为序贯决策问题，引入双智能体范式：扰动智能体（perturbation agent）生成音频扰动，策略智能体（policy agent）在扰动条件下学习选择并执行检测工具；通过对抗学习实现扰动感知的工具选择与自适应执行策略。

Result: 在多个数据集和真实世界损坏（corruptions）上的大量实验表明，ROGUE 在鲁棒性和泛化性上均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 对抗优化的流程生成方法对构建真实部署环境下可靠的音频深度伪造检测系统具有有效性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of speech synthesis and voice conversion technologies has made audio deepfakes increasingly realistic, posing serious security risks in practical applications. While existing detection methods achieve strong performance under controlled conditions, they often fail to generalize under real-world perturbations and corruptions. In this paper, we propose ROGUE, a framework that dynamically constructs robust detection workflows by orchestrating multiple detection tools. ROGUE formulates workflow generation as a sequential decision-making problem and introduces a dual-agent paradigm, where a perturbation agent generates audio perturbations and a policy agent learns to select and execute detection tools under perturbed conditions. Through adversarial learning, ROGUE enables perturbation-aware tool selection, adaptive execution strategies, and improved robustness to distribution shifts. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and real-world corruptions demonstrate that ROGUE consistently outperforms strong baselines in both robustness and generalization. Our results highlight the effectiveness of adversarially optimized workflow generation for building reliable audio deepfake detection systems in real-world deployment settings.

</details>


### [120] [CircleMatch: Prototype Matching with Circular Temporal Statistics for Tiny Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20070)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CircleMatch框架，用极低参数量实现关键词识别（KWS），在多个数据集上取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键词识别是语音设备核心能力，在严格参数预算下跨不同词表规模保持高精度仍具挑战，需要极小型参数量方案。

Method: 编码器独立压缩频带并融合为帧特征，将其与学习的类别专属原型匹配生成时序响应曲线；采用无参数的圆形聚合（circular aggregation），将时间编码为角度，汇总响应分布与相对时序进行分类；设计四个微型变体Circle-D4/D8/D16/D32。

Result: 在12类设定下参数量约1k至7k；在Speech Commands v1/v2及多语言口语词库的英语和西班牙语Micro子集上，多随机种子实验的精度与小型模型相比具竞争力；定性分析显示原型响应具有近似平移等变性并可适应时序压缩。

Conclusion: CircleMatch以极低参数量实现有竞争力的KWS精度，验证了原型匹配与圆形聚合的有效性，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Keyword spotting (KWS), the task of identifying predefined words in speech, is a core capability of voice-enabled devices. Achieving high KWS accuracy under tight parameter budgets across different vocabulary sizes remains challenging. We present CircleMatch, a matching framework enabling KWS with very few parameters. Its encoder independently compresses frequency bands and fuses them into frame features. These features are then matched against learned class-specific prototypes to produce temporal response curves. Parameter-free circular aggregation encodes time as angles and summarizes response distributions and relative timing for classification. We develop four tiny variants, Circle-D4, Circle-D8, Circle-D16, and Circle-D32, ranging from approximately 1k to 7k parameters in the 12-class setting. Experiments with multiple random seeds on Speech Commands v1/v2 and the English and Spanish Micro subsets of the Multilingual Spoken Words Corpus demonstrate competitive accuracy with tiny models. Our qualitative analysis further suggests approximate shift equivariance of prototype responses and adaptation to temporal compression. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/ora942878/CircleMatch.

</details>


### [121] [Multi-Dimensional Prosody Judgment For Live Streaming Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20124)
*Zifan Guan,Longyu Lu,Junan Zhang,Zhizheng Wu,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文针对直播流式TTS细粒度韵律评估需求，提出低成本配对评测器LPJ（由Gemini蒸馏到Qwen3-Omni），并发现多维度评估中的“判定耦合”缺陷，进而提出去耦的D-LPJ方案，在人工标注测试集与Best-of-8候选选择中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播流式TTS需要评估情感、语调、能量等细粒度且高表现力的韵律，传统MOS预测器无法捕捉这些维度；而Gemini等专有LLM虽能评估但成本过高，难以支撑大规模推理与强化学习反馈，因此需要低成本的高质量评测器。

Method: 首先将Gemini蒸馏到Qwen3-Omni，得到低成本配对评测器LPJ；随后识别出标准多维度评估中的“判定耦合”问题（各维度分数懒散地向整体偏好对齐，导致多维评分退化为单一偏好位）。为此提出D-LPJ：移除整体判定目标以避免盲从、在SFT阶段掩蔽不确定的配对-维度、并引入span-local GRPO策略，将归一化优势严格应用于对应的理由片段。

Result: 在精心筛选的人工标注测试集上，10样本、平衡顺序的LPJ点准确率高于单次Gemini调用；D-LPJ能产生独立、去耦的维度判定。在Best-of-8 TTS候选选择锦标赛中，LPJ选出的语句在85.29%的高置信案例中落入人类前三。

Conclusion: LPJ以低成本实现了优于单次Gemini调用的细粒度韵律评估，D-LPJ解决了判定耦合问题并实现维度解耦，整体方法对细粒度TTS偏好优化具有实用效果与推广价值。

Abstract: Evaluating live streaming speech synthesis (TTS) requires assessing fine-grained, highly expressive prosody such as emotion, intonation, and energy which traditional MOS predictors fail to capture. While proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini can evaluate these aspects, they are too costly for massive inference and reinforcement learning feedback. To address this, we first introduce Live-ProsodyJudge (LPJ), a cost-effective pairwise evaluator distilled from Gemini into Qwen3-Omni. However, we identify a critical flaw in standard multi-dimensional evaluation: verdict coupling. The judge tends to lazily align all individual dimension scores with its overall preference, collapsing a rich multi-dimensional rubric into a single preference bit. To resolve this, we further propose Decoupled-Live-ProsodyJudge (D-LPJ). D-LPJ eliminates the overall verdict target to prevent blind following, masks uncertain pair-dimensions during Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT), and introduces a novel span-local GRPO strategy that applies normalized advantages strictly to their corresponding rationale spans. Evaluated on highly curated human-annotated test sets, 10 sample balanced-order LPJ achieves higher point accuracy than a single Gemini call, while D-LPJ successfully produces independent,decoupled dimension judgments. Furthermore, in a Best-of-8 TTS candidate selection tournament, the LPJ-selected utterance falls within the human top-3 in 85.29% of high-confidence cases, demonstrating its efficacy for fine-grained TTS preference optimization.

</details>


### [122] [Music Hallucination in Audio-Language Models: A Hierarchical Formulation and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20195)
*Yu Liu,Jiahui Liu,Zhilin Liu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Yuling Yang,Pin Xu,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文首次针对音频-语言模型的音乐幻觉进行分层、多范式的实证研究，提出五层感知接地失败框架与MuseDiag诊断框架，评测九个模型并发现人声误感知普遍、调性感知区分架构，两种免训练缓解方法仅在探测任务中部分有效且难以迁移到自由生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-语言模型日益生成自信但缺乏音频依据的音乐描述，即音乐领域的幻觉问题。此前缺乏针对音乐、分层、多范式的系统性实证研究，作者希望将音乐幻觉界定为跨五层的层级化感知接地失败，并揭示其成因与缓解途径。

Method: 本文将音乐幻觉形式化为五个层级的感知接地失败（声音事件、时间属性、调性属性、风格、情绪）；提出多范式诊断框架MuseDiag，采用基于矛盾的验证；评测九个模型（四个开源、五个闭源）；并设计两种免训练缓解方法：面向音乐的音频依赖感知解码（ADD-M）与分类学引导的感知锚定（TPA）。

Result: （1）人声误感知是九个模型的普遍弱点，调性感知是架构区分的主要维度，Audio-Flamingo-3保持稳定领先，其下模型排名大幅重排，显示范式特异的脆弱性；（2）肯定偏差、生成模式效应与层级特异的感知局限均与观察模式经验相关，多分析证据趋同而非严格因果归因；（3）ADD-M与TPA可减少探测任务中的幻觉，但增益因模型而异，且常无法迁移到自由生成。

Conclusion: 音乐幻觉缓解必须在多种范式下评估；仅靠探测任务的改善不足以代表自由生成场景的效果。该研究提供了首个音乐特异的层级多范式幻觉分析框架与诊断工具，为后续模型改进与评估设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Audio-language models increasingly generate confident music descriptions that are unsupported by the input audio. We present, to our knowledge, the first music-specific, layer-wise, multi-paradigm empirical study of hallucination in audio-language models and formulate it as a hierarchical perceptual grounding failure across five layers: sound events, temporal properties, tonal attributes, style, and emotion. We introduce MuseDiag, a multi-paradigm diagnostic framework with contradiction-based verification, and evaluate nine models (four open-source and five closed-source). We find that (1) vocal misperception is a universal weakness across all nine models, tonal perception is a major axis of architectural differentiation, and Audio-Flamingo-3 remains the stable leader while substantial reordering below it reveals paradigm-specific vulnerability profiles; (2) affirmative bias, generation-mode effects, and layer-specific perceptual limitations are each empirically associated with the observed patterns, with convergent evidence from multiple analyses rather than strict causal attribution; and (3) our two training-free mitigation methods, Audio-Dependency-Aware Decoding for Music (ADD-M) and Taxonomy-Guided Perceptual Anchoring (TPA), can reduce hallucination in probing, but their gains vary by model and often do not carry over to free-form generation, showing that music hallucination mitigation must be evaluated across paradigms.

</details>
